color-fg-muted mb-3 Lead_container__m3L5f" data-container="lead" data-testid="lead" data-search="lead"><p>Use the REST API to manage security managers in an organization.</p></div><div class="MarkdownContent_markdownBody__v5MYy markdown-body pt-3 pb-4"><h2 id="about-security-managers" tabindex="-1"><a class="heading-link" href="#about-security-managers">About security managers<span class="heading-link-symbol" aria-hidden="true"></span></a></h2>
<p>The security manager role is an organization-level role that organization owners can assign to any member or team in the organization. When applied, it gives permission to view security alerts and manage settings for security features across your organization, as well as read permission for all repositories in the organization.</p></div><div class="MarkdownContent_markdownBody__v5MYy markdown-body pt-3 pb-4"><div class="pb-8"><h2 id="list-security-manager-teams" tabindex="-1"><a class="heading-link" href="#list-security-manager-teams">List security manager teams<span aria-hidden="true" class="heading-link-symbol"></span></a></h2><div class="d-flex flex-wrap gutter mt-4"><div class="col-md-12 col-lg-6"><div class="RestOperation_codeBlock__mf2k2"><div class="ghd-alert ghd-alert-attention" data-container="alert"><p class="ghd-alert-title"><svg version="1.1" width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="octicon mr-2" aria-hidden><path d="M6.457 1.047c.659-1.234 2.427-1.234 3.086 0l6.082 11.378A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.082 15H1.918a1.75 1.75 0 0 1-1.543-2.575Zm1.763.707a.25.25 0 0 0-.44 0L1.698 13.132a.25.25 0 0 0 .22.368h12.164a.25.25 0 0 0 .22-.368Zm.53 3.996v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 11a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Warning</p>
<p>
<strong>Closing down notice:</strong> This operation is closing down and will be removed starting January 1, 2026. Please use the "<a href="https://docs.github.com/enterprise-cloud@latest/rest/orgs/organization-roles">Organization Roles</a>" endpoints instead.</p>
</div></div><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="list-security-manager-teams--fine-grained-access-tokens"><a href="#list-security-manager-teams--fine-grained-access-tokens">Fine-grained access tokens for &quot;List security manager teams&quot;</a></h3><p>This endpoint works with the following fine-grained token types<!-- -->:</p><ul><li><a rel="" href="/en/enterprise-cloud@latest/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-a-user-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App user access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/enterprise-cloud@latest/apps/creating-github-apps/authenticating-with-a-github-app/generating-an-installation-access-token-for-a-github-app">GitHub App installation access tokens</a></li><li><a rel="" href="/en/enterprise-cloud@latest/authentication/keeping-your-account-and-data-secure/managing-your-personal-access-tokens#creating-a-fine-grained-personal-access-token">Fine-grained personal access tokens</a></li></ul><p>The fine-grained token must have the following permission set:</p><ul><li><span><span>&quot;Administration&quot; organization permissions (read)</span></span></li></ul><h3 class="mt-4 mb-3 pt-3 h4" id="list-security-manager-teams--parameters"><a href="#list-security-manager-teams--parameters">Parameters for &quot;List security manager teams&quot;</a></h3><table class="ParameterTable_parameterTable__NVZ8g" summary="Column one describes the parameter.
